Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $\mathfrak{C}\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ be a set of tiles with $\mathfrak{C}=\mathfrak{T}(u_1)$ or $\mathfrak{C}=\mathfrak{T}(u_2)\cap\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ (`t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂`) is the set of tiles $p\in\mathfrak{T}(u_1)\cup\mathfrak{T}(u_2)$ with $2^{Zn/2}\le d_{\mathfrak{c}(p),\,D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)}/4}(\mathcal{Q}(u_1),\mathcal{Q}(u_2))$, where $Z:=$ `defaultZ a` $=2^{12a}\in\mathbb{N}$ and $Zn/2$ is computed in $\mathbb{R}$. Let $J\in$ `Grid X` be a cube with $J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). Let $f:X\to\mathbb{C}$ be a function that has `BoundedCompactSupport` with respect to $\mu$ (per its docstring: is a bounded, compactly supported, measurable function). Notation: for a tile $p'\in\mathfrak{P}$, a function $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $T^*_{p'} g(z)\in\mathbb{C}$ (`adjointCarleson p' g z`) $=\int_{E(p')}\overline{K_{\mathfrak{s}(p')}(y,z)}\,e^{i(Q(y)(y)-Q(y)(z))}\,g(y)\,d\mu(y)$, where $E(p')=\{y\in X: y\in\mathcal{I}(p'),\ Q(y)\in\Omega(p'),\ \sigma_1(y)\le\mathfrak{s}(p')\le\sigma_2(y)\}$ (`E p'`), $\Omega(p')\subseteq\Theta(X)$ is the set `Ω p'` of the tile structure, and $K_m(y,z)=K(y,z)\,\psi(D^{-m}d(y,z))$ for $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ and $y,z\in X$ (`Ks`), with $\psi$ the project's function `ψ` (taken with parameter $D$); for a set $\mathfrak{C}'\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ of tiles, $T^*_{\mathfrak{C}'}g(z)=\sum_{p'\in\mathfrak{C}'}T^*_{p'} g(z)$ (`adjointCarlesonSum`); for $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $Mg(z)\in[0,\infty]$ (`maximalFunction μ Forest.𝓑 Forest.c𝓑 Forest.r𝓑 1 g z`) $=\sup_{(\ell_1,\ell_2,I')\in\mathcal{B}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}(z)\,⨍_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}\|g(y)\|_e\,d\mu(y)$, where $\mathcal{B}=\{0,\dots,S+5\}\times\{0,\dots,2S+3\}\times$ `Grid X` $\subseteq\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N}\times$ `Grid X`, $B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}=B(c(I'),\,2^{\ell_1}D^{s(I')+\ell_2})$, and $⨍$ is the average over the ball with respect to $\mu$ (lower integral divided by the measure of the ball, in $[0,\infty]$); $C_{7.5.9s}(a)\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ is the constant `Forest.C7_5_9s a` (per its docstring, the constant used in `global_tree_control1_supbound`, with value $2^{128a^3+4a+3}$ in the blueprint). Then, in $[0,\infty]$, $$\sup_{x\in B(c(J),16D^{s(J)})}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{C}}f(x)\|_e\le\inf_{x\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{C}}f(x)\|_e+C_{7.5.9s}(a)\inf_{y\in J}Mf(y).$$

### `TileStructure.Forest.limited_scale_impact_second_estimate`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hp : p ∈ (fun x => t.𝔗 x) u₂ \ t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂) (hJ : J ∈ t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂) (h : ¬Disjoint (Metric.ball (𝔠 p) (8 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ 𝔰 p)) (Metric.ball (c J) (8⁻¹ * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J))) : 𝔰 p ≤ s J + 3`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ p : 𝔓 X} {J : Grid X}, p ∈ (fun (x : 𝔓 X) => t.𝔗 (F := F) (G := G) x) u₂ \ t.𝔖₀ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ → J ∈ t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ → ¬Disjoint (Metric.ball (𝔠 p) ((8 : ℝ) * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (𝔰 p))) (Metric.ball (c J) ((8 : ℝ)⁻¹ * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J))) → 𝔰 p ≤ s J + (3 : ℤ)`
Docstring: Part of Lemma 7.5.6.
English: (Part of Lemma 7.5.6, per the docstring.) Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2,p\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $p\in\mathfrak{T}(u_2)\setminus\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ (`t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂`) is the set of tiles $p\in\mathfrak{T}(u_1)\cup\mathfrak{T}(u_2)$ with $2^{Zn/2}\le d_{\mathfrak{c}(p),\,D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)}/4}(\mathcal{Q}(u_1),\mathcal{Q}(u_2))$, where $Z:=$ `defaultZ a` $=2^{12a}\in\mathbb{N}$ and $Zn/2$ is computed in $\mathbb{R}$. Let $J\in$ `Grid X` be a cube with $J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). If the balls $B(\mathfrak{c}(p),8D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)})$ and $B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)$ are not disjoint, then $\mathfrak{s}(p)\le s(J)+3$.

### `TileStructure.Forest.local_tree_control_sumsumsup`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hu₁ : u₁ ∈ t) (hu₂ : u₂ ∈ t) (hu : u₁ ≠ u₂) (h2u : 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂) (hJ : J ∈ t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂) : ⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) (8⁻¹ * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J), ‖adjointCarlesonSum ((fun x => t.𝔗 x) u₂ \ t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂) f x‖ₑ ≤ ∑ k ∈ Finset.Icc (s J) (s J + 3), ∑ p with 𝔰 p = k ∧ ¬Disjoint (Metric.ball (𝔠 p) (8 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ 𝔰 p)) (Metric.ball (c J) (8⁻¹ * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J)), ⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) (8⁻¹ * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J), ‖adjointCarleson p f x‖ₑ`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ : 𝔓 X} {f : X → ℂ} {J : Grid X}, u₁ ∈ t → u₂ ∈ t → u₁ ≠ u₂ → 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂ → J ∈ t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ → LE.le (α := ENNReal) (⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) ((8 : ℝ)⁻¹ * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J)), ‖adjointCarlesonSum (F := F) (G := G) ((fun (x : 𝔓 X) => t.𝔗 (F := F) (G := G) x) u₂ \ t.𝔖₀ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂) f x‖ₑ) (∑ k ∈ Finset.Icc (s J) (s J + (3 : ℤ)), ∑ p : 𝔓 X with 𝔰 p = k ∧ ¬Disjoint (Metric.ball (𝔠 p) ((8 : ℝ) * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (𝔰 p))) (Metric.ball (c J) ((8 : ℝ)⁻¹ * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J))), ⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) ((8 : ℝ)⁻¹ * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J)), ‖adjointCarleson (F := F) (G := G) p f x‖ₑ)`
English: Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $J\in$ `Grid X` be a cube with $J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). Let $f:X\to\mathbb{C}$ be any function. Notation: $\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ (`t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂`) is the set of tiles $p\in\mathfrak{T}(u_1)\cup\mathfrak{T}(u_2)$ with $2^{Zn/2}\le d_{\mathfrak{c}(p),\,D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)}/4}(\mathcal{Q}(u_1),\mathcal{Q}(u_2))$, where $Z:=$ `defaultZ a` $=2^{12a}\in\mathbb{N}$ and $Zn/2$ is computed in $\mathbb{R}$; for a tile $p'\in\mathfrak{P}$, a function $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $T^*_{p'} g(z)\in\mathbb{C}$ (`adjointCarleson p' g z`) $=\int_{E(p')}\overline{K_{\mathfrak{s}(p')}(y,z)}\,e^{i(Q(y)(y)-Q(y)(z))}\,g(y)\,d\mu(y)$, where $E(p')=\{y\in X: y\in\mathcal{I}(p'),\ Q(y)\in\Omega(p'),\ \sigma_1(y)\le\mathfrak{s}(p')\le\sigma_2(y)\}$ (`E p'`), $\Omega(p')\subseteq\Theta(X)$ is the set `Ω p'` of the tile structure, and $K_m(y,z)=K(y,z)\,\psi(D^{-m}d(y,z))$ for $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ and $y,z\in X$ (`Ks`), with $\psi$ the project's function `ψ` (taken with parameter $D$); for a set $\mathfrak{C}'\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ of tiles, $T^*_{\mathfrak{C}'}g(z)=\sum_{p'\in\mathfrak{C}'}T^*_{p'} g(z)$ (`adjointCarlesonSum`). Then, in $[0,\infty]$, $$\sup_{x\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_2)\setminus\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)}f(x)\|_e\le\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z},\ s(J)\le k\le s(J)+3}\ \sum_{\substack{p\in\mathfrak{P}:\ \mathfrak{s}(p)=k,\\ B(\mathfrak{c}(p),8D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)})\cap B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)\ne\emptyset}}\ \sup_{x\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_p f(x)\|_e.$$

### `TileStructure.Forest.local_tree_control`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hu₁ : u₁ ∈ t) (hu₂ : u₂ ∈ t) (hu : u₁ ≠ u₂) (h2u : 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂) (hJ : J ∈ t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂) (hf : BoundedCompactSupport f volume) : ⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) (8⁻¹ * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J), ‖adjointCarlesonSum ((fun x => t.𝔗 x) u₂ \ t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂) f x‖ₑ ≤ ↑(Forest.C7_5_7 a) * ⨅ x ∈ J, maximalFunction volume Forest.𝓑 Forest.c𝓑 Forest.r𝓑 1 f x`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ : 𝔓 X} {f : X → ℂ} {J : Grid X}, u₁ ∈ t → u₂ ∈ t → u₁ ≠ u₂ → 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂ → J ∈ t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ → BoundedCompactSupport f volume → ⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) ((8 : ℝ)⁻¹ * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J)), ‖adjointCarlesonSum (F := F) (G := G) ((fun (x : 𝔓 X) => t.𝔗 (F := F) (G := G) x) u₂ \ t.𝔖₀ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂) f x‖ₑ ≤ ↑(Forest.C7_5_7 a) * ⨅ x ∈ J, maximalFunction volume (Forest.𝓑 (F := F) (G := G)) (Forest.c𝓑 (F := F) (G := G)) (Forest.r𝓑 (F := F) (G := G)) (1 : ℝ) f x`
Docstring: Lemma 7.5.7.
English: (Lemma 7.5.7) Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $J\in$ `Grid X` be a cube with $J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). Let $f:X\to\mathbb{C}$ be a function that has `BoundedCompactSupport` with respect to $\mu$ (per its docstring: is a bounded, compactly supported, measurable function). Notation: $\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ (`t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂`) is the set of tiles $p\in\mathfrak{T}(u_1)\cup\mathfrak{T}(u_2)$ with $2^{Zn/2}\le d_{\mathfrak{c}(p),\,D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)}/4}(\mathcal{Q}(u_1),\mathcal{Q}(u_2))$, where $Z:=$ `defaultZ a` $=2^{12a}\in\mathbb{N}$ and $Zn/2$ is computed in $\mathbb{R}$; for a tile $p'\in\mathfrak{P}$, a function $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $T^*_{p'} g(z)\in\mathbb{C}$ (`adjointCarleson p' g z`) $=\int_{E(p')}\overline{K_{\mathfrak{s}(p')}(y,z)}\,e^{i(Q(y)(y)-Q(y)(z))}\,g(y)\,d\mu(y)$, where $E(p')=\{y\in X: y\in\mathcal{I}(p'),\ Q(y)\in\Omega(p'),\ \sigma_1(y)\le\mathfrak{s}(p')\le\sigma_2(y)\}$ (`E p'`), $\Omega(p')\subseteq\Theta(X)$ is the set `Ω p'` of the tile structure, and $K_m(y,z)=K(y,z)\,\psi(D^{-m}d(y,z))$ for $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ and $y,z\in X$ (`Ks`), with $\psi$ the project's function `ψ` (taken with parameter $D$); for a set $\mathfrak{C}'\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ of tiles, $T^*_{\mathfrak{C}'}g(z)=\sum_{p'\in\mathfrak{C}'}T^*_{p'} g(z)$ (`adjointCarlesonSum`); for $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $Mg(z)\in[0,\infty]$ (`maximalFunction μ Forest.𝓑 Forest.c𝓑 Forest.r𝓑 1 g z`) $=\sup_{(\ell_1,\ell_2,I')\in\mathcal{B}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}(z)\,⨍_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}\|g(y)\|_e\,d\mu(y)$, where $\mathcal{B}=\{0,\dots,S+5\}\times\{0,\dots,2S+3\}\times$ `Grid X` $\subseteq\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N}\times$ `Grid X`, $B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}=B(c(I'),\,2^{\ell_1}D^{s(I')+\ell_2})$, and $⨍$ is the average over the ball with respect to $\mu$ (lower integral divided by the measure of the ball, in $[0,\infty]$); $C_{7.5.7}(a)\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ is the constant `Forest.C7_5_7 a` (per its docstring, the constant used in `local_tree_control`, with value $2^{104a^3}$ in the blueprint). Then, in $[0,\infty]$, $$\sup_{x\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_2)\setminus\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)}f(x)\|_e\le C_{7.5.7}(a)\inf_{y\in J}Mf(y).$$

### `TileStructure.Forest.global_tree_control2`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hu₁ : u₁ ∈ t) (hu₂ : u₂ ∈ t) (hu : u₁ ≠ u₂) (h2u : 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂) (hJ : J ∈ t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂) (hf : BoundedCompactSupport f volume) : ⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) (16 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J), ‖adjointCarlesonSum ((fun x => t.𝔗 x) u₂ ∩ t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂) f x‖ₑ ≤ (⨅ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) (8⁻¹ * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J), ‖adjointCarlesonSum ((fun x => t.𝔗 x) u₂) f x‖ₑ) + ↑(Forest.C7_5_10 a) * ⨅ x ∈ J, maximalFunction volume Forest.𝓑 Forest.c𝓑 Forest.r𝓑 1 f x`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ : 𝔓 X} {f : X → ℂ} {J : Grid X}, u₁ ∈ t → u₂ ∈ t → u₁ ≠ u₂ → 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂ → J ∈ t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ → BoundedCompactSupport f volume → ⨆ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) ((16 : ℝ) * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J)), ‖adjointCarlesonSum (F := F) (G := G) ((fun (x : 𝔓 X) => t.𝔗 (F := F) (G := G) x) u₂ ∩ t.𝔖₀ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂) f x‖ₑ ≤ (⨅ x ∈ Metric.ball (c J) ((8 : ℝ)⁻¹ * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J)), ‖adjointCarlesonSum (F := F) (G := G) ((fun (x : 𝔓 X) => t.𝔗 (F := F) (G := G) x) u₂) f x‖ₑ) + ↑(Forest.C7_5_10 a) * ⨅ x ∈ J, maximalFunction volume (Forest.𝓑 (F := F) (G := G)) (Forest.c𝓑 (F := F) (G := G)) (Forest.r𝓑 (F := F) (G := G)) (1 : ℝ) f x`
Docstring: Lemma 7.5.10
English: (Lemma 7.5.10) Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $J\in$ `Grid X` be a cube with $J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). Let $f:X\to\mathbb{C}$ be a function that has `BoundedCompactSupport` with respect to $\mu$ (per its docstring: is a bounded, compactly supported, measurable function). Notation: $\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ (`t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂`) is the set of tiles $p\in\mathfrak{T}(u_1)\cup\mathfrak{T}(u_2)$ with $2^{Zn/2}\le d_{\mathfrak{c}(p),\,D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)}/4}(\mathcal{Q}(u_1),\mathcal{Q}(u_2))$, where $Z:=$ `defaultZ a` $=2^{12a}\in\mathbb{N}$ and $Zn/2$ is computed in $\mathbb{R}$; for a tile $p'\in\mathfrak{P}$, a function $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $T^*_{p'} g(z)\in\mathbb{C}$ (`adjointCarleson p' g z`) $=\int_{E(p')}\overline{K_{\mathfrak{s}(p')}(y,z)}\,e^{i(Q(y)(y)-Q(y)(z))}\,g(y)\,d\mu(y)$, where $E(p')=\{y\in X: y\in\mathcal{I}(p'),\ Q(y)\in\Omega(p'),\ \sigma_1(y)\le\mathfrak{s}(p')\le\sigma_2(y)\}$ (`E p'`), $\Omega(p')\subseteq\Theta(X)$ is the set `Ω p'` of the tile structure, and $K_m(y,z)=K(y,z)\,\psi(D^{-m}d(y,z))$ for $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ and $y,z\in X$ (`Ks`), with $\psi$ the project's function `ψ` (taken with parameter $D$); for a set $\mathfrak{C}'\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ of tiles, $T^*_{\mathfrak{C}'}g(z)=\sum_{p'\in\mathfrak{C}'}T^*_{p'} g(z)$ (`adjointCarlesonSum`); for $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $Mg(z)\in[0,\infty]$ (`maximalFunction μ Forest.𝓑 Forest.c𝓑 Forest.r𝓑 1 g z`) $=\sup_{(\ell_1,\ell_2,I')\in\mathcal{B}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}(z)\,⨍_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}\|g(y)\|_e\,d\mu(y)$, where $\mathcal{B}=\{0,\dots,S+5\}\times\{0,\dots,2S+3\}\times$ `Grid X` $\subseteq\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N}\times$ `Grid X`, $B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}=B(c(I'),\,2^{\ell_1}D^{s(I')+\ell_2})$, and $⨍$ is the average over the ball with respect to $\mu$ (lower integral divided by the measure of the ball, in $[0,\infty]$); $C_{7.5.10}(a)\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ is the constant `Forest.C7_5_10 a` (per its docstring, the constant used in `global_tree_control2`, with value $2^{129a^3}$ in the blueprint). Then, in $[0,\infty]$, $$\sup_{x\in B(c(J),16D^{s(J)})}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_2)\cap\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)}f(x)\|_e\le\inf_{x\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_2)}f(x)\|_e+C_{7.5.10}(a)\inf_{y\in J}Mf(y).$$

### `TileStructure.Forest.union_𝓙₅`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hu₁ : u₁ ∈ t) (hu₂ : u₂ ∈ t) (hu : u₁ ≠ u₂) (h2u : 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂) : ⋃ J ∈ t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂, ↑J = ↑(𝓘 u₁)`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ : 𝔓 X}, u₁ ∈ t → u₂ ∈ t → u₁ ≠ u₂ → 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂ → ⋃ J ∈ t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂, ↑J = ↑(𝓘 u₁)`
Docstring: Part of Lemma 7.5.1.
English: (Part of Lemma 7.5.1, per the docstring.) Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). Then the union of the cubes in $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, viewed as subsets of $X$, equals $\mathcal{I}(u_1)$ (as a subset of $X$): $\bigcup_{J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)}J=\mathcal{I}(u_1)$.

Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Let $x\in X$ with $x\in B(c(J),16D^{s(J)})$. Notation: for a tile $p'\in\mathfrak{P}$, a function $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $T^*_{p'} g(z)\in\mathbb{C}$ (`adjointCarleson p' g z`) $=\int_{E(p')}\overline{K_{\mathfrak{s}(p')}(y,z)}\,e^{i(Q(y)(y)-Q(y)(z))}\,g(y)\,d\mu(y)$, where $E(p')=\{y\in X: y\in\mathcal{I}(p'),\ Q(y)\in\Omega(p'),\ \sigma_1(y)\le\mathfrak{s}(p')\le\sigma_2(y)\}$ (`E p'`), $\Omega(p')\subseteq\Theta(X)$ is the set `Ω p'` of the tile structure, and $K_m(y,z)=K(y,z)\,\psi(D^{-m}d(y,z))$ for $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ and $y,z\in X$ (`Ks`), with $\psi$ the project's function `ψ` (taken with parameter $D$); for a set $\mathfrak{C}'\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ of tiles, $T^*_{\mathfrak{C}'}g(z)=\sum_{p'\in\mathfrak{C}'}T^*_{p'} g(z)$ (`adjointCarlesonSum`); for $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $Mg(z)\in[0,\infty]$ (`maximalFunction μ Forest.𝓑 Forest.c𝓑 Forest.r𝓑 1 g z`) $=\sup_{(\ell_1,\ell_2,I')\in\mathcal{B}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}(z)\,⨍_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}\|g(y)\|_e\,d\mu(y)$, where $\mathcal{B}=\{0,\dots,S+5\}\times\{0,\dots,2S+3\}\times$ `Grid X` $\subseteq\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N}\times$ `Grid X`, $B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}=B(c(I'),\,2^{\ell_1}D^{s(I')+\ell_2})$, and $⨍$ is the average over the ball with respect to $\mu$ (lower integral divided by the measure of the ball, in $[0,\infty]$); $h_J:X\to\mathbb{C}$ denotes the function `t.holderFunction u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J` (per its docstring, the function $h_J$ defined in the proof of Section 7.5.2); $P(J)\in[0,\infty]$ (`t.P7_5_4 u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J`, per its docstring the product on the right-hand side of Lemma 7.5.4) $=\big(\inf_{y\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_1)}f_1(y)\|_e+\inf_{y\in J}Mf_1(y)\big)\cdot\big(\inf_{y\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_2)}f_2(y)\|_e+\inf_{y\in J}Mf_2(y)\big)$; $C_{7.5.9s}(a)\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ is the constant `Forest.C7_5_9s a` (per its docstring, the constant used in `global_tree_control1_supbound`, with value $2^{128a^3+4a+3}$ in the blueprint); $C_{7.5.10}(a)\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ is the constant `Forest.C7_5_10 a` (per its docstring, the constant used in `global_tree_control2`, with value $2^{129a^3}$ in the blueprint). Then, in $[0,\infty]$, $$\|h_J(x)\|_e\le C_{7.5.9s}(a)\,C_{7.5.10}(a)\,P(J).$$

### `TileStructure.Forest.holder_correlation_tree`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hu₁ : u₁ ∈ t) (hu₂ : u₂ ∈ t) (hu : u₁ ≠ u₂) (h2u : 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂) (hJ : J ∈ t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂) (hf₁ : BoundedCompactSupport f₁ volume) (hf₂ : BoundedCompactSupport f₂ volume) : iHolENorm (t.holderFunction u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J) (c J) (16 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J) (defaultτ a) ≤ ↑(Forest.C7_5_4 a) * t.P7_5_4 u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ : 𝔓 X} {f₁ f₂ : X → ℂ} {J : Grid X}, u₁ ∈ t → u₂ ∈ t → u₁ ≠ u₂ → 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂ → J ∈ t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ → BoundedCompactSupport f₁ volume → BoundedCompactSupport f₂ volume → iHolENorm (t.holderFunction (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J) (c J) ((16 : ℝ) * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J)) (defaultτ a) ≤ ↑(Forest.C7_5_4 a) * t.P7_5_4 (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J`
Docstring: Lemma 7.5.4.
English: (Lemma 7.5.4) Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $J\in$ `Grid X` be a cube with $J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). Let $f_1,f_2:X\to\mathbb{C}$ be functions that each has `BoundedCompactSupport` with respect to $\mu$ (per its docstring: each is a bounded, compactly supported, measurable function). Notation: for a tile $p'\in\mathfrak{P}$, a function $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $T^*_{p'} g(z)\in\mathbb{C}$ (`adjointCarleson p' g z`) $=\int_{E(p')}\overline{K_{\mathfrak{s}(p')}(y,z)}\,e^{i(Q(y)(y)-Q(y)(z))}\,g(y)\,d\mu(y)$, where $E(p')=\{y\in X: y\in\mathcal{I}(p'),\ Q(y)\in\Omega(p'),\ \sigma_1(y)\le\mathfrak{s}(p')\le\sigma_2(y)\}$ (`E p'`), $\Omega(p')\subseteq\Theta(X)$ is the set `Ω p'` of the tile structure, and $K_m(y,z)=K(y,z)\,\psi(D^{-m}d(y,z))$ for $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ and $y,z\in X$ (`Ks`), with $\psi$ the project's function `ψ` (taken with parameter $D$); for a set $\mathfrak{C}'\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ of tiles, $T^*_{\mathfrak{C}'}g(z)=\sum_{p'\in\mathfrak{C}'}T^*_{p'} g(z)$ (`adjointCarlesonSum`); for $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $Mg(z)\in[0,\infty]$ (`maximalFunction μ Forest.𝓑 Forest.c𝓑 Forest.r𝓑 1 g z`) $=\sup_{(\ell_1,\ell_2,I')\in\mathcal{B}}\mathbf{1}_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}(z)\,⨍_{B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}}\|g(y)\|_e\,d\mu(y)$, where $\mathcal{B}=\{0,\dots,S+5\}\times\{0,\dots,2S+3\}\times$ `Grid X` $\subseteq\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N}\times$ `Grid X`, $B_{\ell_1,\ell_2,I'}=B(c(I'),\,2^{\ell_1}D^{s(I')+\ell_2})$, and $⨍$ is the average over the ball with respect to $\mu$ (lower integral divided by the measure of the ball, in $[0,\infty]$); $h_J:X\to\mathbb{C}$ denotes the function `t.holderFunction u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J` (per its docstring, the function $h_J$ defined in the proof of Section 7.5.2); $P(J)\in[0,\infty]$ (`t.P7_5_4 u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J`, per its docstring the product on the right-hand side of Lemma 7.5.4) $=\big(\inf_{y\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_1)}f_1(y)\|_e+\inf_{y\in J}Mf_1(y)\big)\cdot\big(\inf_{y\in B(c(J),D^{s(J)}/8)}\|T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_2)}f_2(y)\|_e+\inf_{y\in J}Mf_2(y)\big)$; for $\varphi:X\to\mathbb{C}$, $y_0\in X$ and $R,\tau'\in\mathbb{R}$, $\|\varphi\|_{C^{\tau'}(y_0,R)}\in[0,\infty]$ (`iHolENorm φ y₀ R τ'`, per its docstring the $L^\infty$-normalized $\tau'$-Hölder norm) $=\sup_{y\in B(y_0,R)}\|\varphi(y)\|_e+\mathrm{ofReal}(R)^{\tau'}\sup_{y,z\in B(y_0,R),\,y\ne z}\|\varphi(y)-\varphi(z)\|_e/\bar d(y,z)^{\tau'}$, where $\mathrm{ofReal}(R)=\max(R,0)\in[0,\infty]$; $\tau:=$ `defaultτ a` $=(a:\mathbb{R})^{-1}$; $C_{7.5.4}(a)\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ is the constant `Forest.C7_5_4 a` (per its docstring, the constant used in `holder_correlation_tree`, with value $2^{485a^3}$ in the blueprint). Then, in $[0,\infty]$, $$\|h_J\|_{C^{\tau}(c(J),\,16D^{s(J)})}\le C_{7.5.4}(a)\,P(J).$$

### `TileStructure.Forest.cdtp_le_iHolENorm`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hu₁ : u₁ ∈ t) (hu₂ : u₂ ∈ t) (hu : u₁ ≠ u₂) (h2u : 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂) (hf₁ : BoundedCompactSupport f₁ volume) (hf₂ : BoundedCompactSupport f₂ volume) : ‖∫ (x : X), adjointCarlesonSum ((fun x => t.𝔗 x) u₁) f₁ x * (starRingEnd ℂ) (adjointCarlesonSum ((fun x => t.𝔗 x) u₂ ∩ t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂) f₂ x)‖ₑ ≤ ∑ J ∈ (t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂).toFinset, ↑(C2_0_5 ↑a) * volume (Metric.ball (c J) (8 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J)) * iHolENorm (t.holderFunction u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J) (c J) (2 * (8 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J)) (defaultτ a) * (1 + edist_{c J, 8 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J} (𝒬 u₂) (𝒬 u₁)) ^ (-(2 * ↑a ^ 2 + ↑a ^ 3)⁻¹)`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ : 𝔓 X} {f₁ f₂ : X → ℂ}, u₁ ∈ t → u₂ ∈ t → u₁ ≠ u₂ → 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂ → BoundedCompactSupport f₁ volume → BoundedCompactSupport f₂ volume → enorm (E := ℂ) (@integral _ _ _ _ MeasureSpace.toMeasurableSpace volume fun (x : X) => HMul.hMul (β := ℂ) (adjointCarlesonSum (F := F) (G := G) ((fun (x : 𝔓 X) => t.𝔗 (F := F) (G := G) x) u₁) f₁ x) ((starRingEnd ℂ) (adjointCarlesonSum (F := F) (G := G) ((fun (x : 𝔓 X) => t.𝔗 (F := F) (G := G) x) u₂ ∩ t.𝔖₀ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂) f₂ x) : ℂ)) ≤ ∑ J ∈ (t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂).toFinset, HMul.hMul (β := ENNReal) ↑(C2_0_5 ↑a) ((volume : Measure X) (Metric.ball (c J) ((8 : ℝ) * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J))) : ENNReal) * iHolENorm (t.holderFunction (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J) (c J) ((2 : ℝ) * ((8 : ℝ) * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) (↑(defaultD a)) (s J))) (defaultτ a) * ((1 : ENNReal) + edist_{c J, (8 : ℝ) * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J} (𝒬 u₂) (𝒬 u₁)) ^ (-((2 : ℝ) * HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) ↑a (2 : ℕ) + HPow.hPow (α := ℝ) ↑a (3 : ℕ))⁻¹)`
English: Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $f_1,f_2:X\to\mathbb{C}$ be functions that each has `BoundedCompactSupport` with respect to $\mu$ (per its docstring: each is a bounded, compactly supported, measurable function). Notation: $\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ (`t.𝔖₀ u₁ u₂`) is the set of tiles $p\in\mathfrak{T}(u_1)\cup\mathfrak{T}(u_2)$ with $2^{Zn/2}\le d_{\mathfrak{c}(p),\,D^{\mathfrak{s}(p)}/4}(\mathcal{Q}(u_1),\mathcal{Q}(u_2))$, where $Z:=$ `defaultZ a` $=2^{12a}\in\mathbb{N}$ and $Zn/2$ is computed in $\mathbb{R}$; $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1); for a tile $p'\in\mathfrak{P}$, a function $g:X\to\mathbb{C}$ and $z\in X$, $T^*_{p'} g(z)\in\mathbb{C}$ (`adjointCarleson p' g z`) $=\int_{E(p')}\overline{K_{\mathfrak{s}(p')}(y,z)}\,e^{i(Q(y)(y)-Q(y)(z))}\,g(y)\,d\mu(y)$, where $E(p')=\{y\in X: y\in\mathcal{I}(p'),\ Q(y)\in\Omega(p'),\ \sigma_1(y)\le\mathfrak{s}(p')\le\sigma_2(y)\}$ (`E p'`), $\Omega(p')\subseteq\Theta(X)$ is the set `Ω p'` of the tile structure, and $K_m(y,z)=K(y,z)\,\psi(D^{-m}d(y,z))$ for $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ and $y,z\in X$ (`Ks`), with $\psi$ the project's function `ψ` (taken with parameter $D$); for a set $\mathfrak{C}'\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ of tiles, $T^*_{\mathfrak{C}'}g(z)=\sum_{p'\in\mathfrak{C}'}T^*_{p'} g(z)$ (`adjointCarlesonSum`); for each cube $J\in$ `Grid X`, $h_J:X\to\mathbb{C}$ denotes the function `t.holderFunction u₁ u₂ f₁ f₂ J` (per its docstring, the function $h_J$ defined in the proof of Section 7.5.2); for $\varphi:X\to\mathbb{C}$, $y_0\in X$ and $R,\tau'\in\mathbb{R}$, $\|\varphi\|_{C^{\tau'}(y_0,R)}\in[0,\infty]$ (`iHolENorm φ y₀ R τ'`, per its docstring the $L^\infty$-normalized $\tau'$-Hölder norm) $=\sup_{y\in B(y_0,R)}\|\varphi(y)\|_e+\mathrm{ofReal}(R)^{\tau'}\sup_{y,z\in B(y_0,R),\,y\ne z}\|\varphi(y)-\varphi(z)\|_e/\bar d(y,z)^{\tau'}$, where $\mathrm{ofReal}(R)=\max(R,0)\in[0,\infty]$; $\tau:=$ `defaultτ a` $=(a:\mathbb{R})^{-1}$; $C_{2.0.5}(a)=$ `C2_0_5 a` $=2^{7a}\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ (with $a$ viewed as a real number). Then, in $[0,\infty]$, $$\Big\|\int_X T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_1)}f_1(x)\,\overline{T^*_{\mathfrak{T}(u_2)\cap\mathfrak{S}_0(u_1,u_2)}f_2(x)}\,d\mu(x)\Big\|_e\le\sum_{J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)}C_{2.0.5}(a)\,\mu\big(B(c(J),8D^{s(J)})\big)\,\|h_J\|_{C^{\tau}(c(J),\,2\cdot 8D^{s(J)})}\,\Big(1+\bar d_{c(J),\,8D^{s(J)}}\big(\mathcal{Q}(u_2),\mathcal{Q}(u_1)\big)\Big)^{-1/(2a^2+a^3)},$$ where the sum runs over the finite set $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ and the exponent $-(2a^2+a^3)^{-1}$ is a real number.

### `TileStructure.Forest.lower_oscillation_bound`
Lean (short): `[ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [TileStructure Q (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X) (cancelPt X)] (hu₁ : u₁ ∈ t) (hu₂ : u₂ ∈ t) (hu : u₁ ≠ u₂) (h2u : 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂) (hJ : J ∈ t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂) : ↑(Forest.C7_5_11 a n) ≤ dist_{c J, 8 * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J} (𝒬 u₁) (𝒬 u₂)`
Lean (full): `∀ {X : Type u_1} {a : ℕ} {q : ℝ} {K : X → X → ℂ} {σ₁ σ₂ : X → ℤ} {F G : Set X} [inst : MetricSpace X] [inst_1 : ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G] [inst_2 : TileStructure (Q (F := F) (G := G)) (defaultD a) (defaultκ a) (defaultS X (F := F) (G := G)) (cancelPt X)] {n : ℕ} {t : Forest X (F := F) (G := G) n} {u₁ u₂ : 𝔓 X} {J : Grid X}, u₁ ∈ t → u₂ ∈ t → u₁ ≠ u₂ → 𝓘 u₁ ≤ 𝓘 u₂ → J ∈ t.𝓙₅ (F := F) (G := G) u₁ u₂ → ↑(Forest.C7_5_11 a n) ≤ dist_{c J, (8 : ℝ) * ↑(defaultD a) ^ s J} (𝒬 u₁) (𝒬 u₂)`
Docstring: Lemma 7.5.11
English: (Lemma 7.5.11) Let $X$ be a metric space. Let $a\in\mathbb{N}$, $q\in\mathbb{R}$, $K : X\to X\to\mathbb{C}$, $\sigma_1,\sigma_2 : X\to\mathbb{Z}$ and $F,G\subseteq X$, and suppose given a `ProofData a q K σ₁ σ₂ F G` structure on $X$ (per its docstring, data common through most of Chapters 2–7). Through its `KernelProofData` component it provides a `DoublingMeasure X (2^a)` structure, hence a measure on $X$, denoted $\mu$ (Lean `volume`), and a `CompatibleFunctions ℝ X (2^a)` structure, hence a `FunctionDistances ℝ X` structure with its type $\Theta(X)$ (per its docstring, a type of continuous functions $X\to\mathbb{R}$; each $\vartheta\in\Theta(X)$ is evaluated as a real function on $X$ via the coercion `coeΘ`); it also provides a simple function $Q : X\to\Theta(X)$ (`ProofData.Q`). For $y\in X$ and $r\in\mathbb{R}$, $d_{y,r}$ denotes the pseudometric on $\Theta(X)$ that this `FunctionDistances` structure attaches to $(y,r)$ (Lean `dist_{y, r}`) and $\bar d_{y,r}$ its extended version (`edist_{y, r}`). Put $D :=$ `defaultD a` $=2^{𝕔 a^2}\in\mathbb{N}$, where $𝕔$ is the project's natural-number constant `𝕔`, and $S :=$ `defaultS X` $\in\mathbb{N}$. Suppose further given a `TileStructure` with parameters $Q$, $D$, `defaultκ a` $=2^{-10a}\in\mathbb{R}$, $S$ and the point `cancelPt X` $\in X$. Let $\mathfrak{P}$ (`𝔓 X`) be its finite type of tiles and `Grid X` its type of cubes (per its docstring, the indexing type of the grid structure, whose elements are called dyadic cubes); each cube $J$ is identified with a subset of $X$ (so $x\in J$ means membership in that subset), and has a centre $c(J)\in X$ and a scale $s(J)\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`c J`, `s J`); cubes are ordered by $J\le J'$ iff $J\subseteq J'$ as subsets of $X$ and $s(J)\le s(J')$. For a tile $p\in\mathfrak{P}$, let $\mathcal{I}(p)\in$ `Grid X` (`𝓘 p`) be its cube, $\mathfrak{c}(p):=c(\mathcal{I}(p))\in X$ and $\mathfrak{s}(p):=s(\mathcal{I}(p))\in\mathbb{Z}$ (`𝔠 p`, `𝔰 p`), and $\mathcal{Q}(p)\in\Theta(X)$ the element `𝒬 p`. Let $n\in\mathbb{N}$ and let $t$ be an $n$-forest (`Forest X n`); for a tile $u$, \"$u\in t$\" means $u\in\mathfrak{U}(t)$ (the set of tiles `t.𝔘`), and $\mathfrak{T}(u)\subseteq\mathfrak{P}$ denotes the set of tiles `t.𝔗 u`. Write $\|\cdot\|_e$ for the extended norm (values in $[0,\infty]$), $\bar d$ for the extended distance (on $X$ and on $\mathbb{C}$, values in $[0,\infty]$), $d$ for the distance on $X$, and $B(y,r)$ for the open ball in $X$ with centre $y\in X$ and radius $r\in\mathbb{R}$. Let $u_1,u_2\in\mathfrak{P}$ be tiles with $u_1\in t$, $u_2\in t$, $u_1\ne u_2$ and $\mathcal{I}(u_1)\le\mathcal{I}(u_2)$. Let $J\in$ `Grid X` be a cube with $J\in\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$, where $\mathcal{J}_5(u_1,u_2)$ denotes the set of cubes `t.𝓙₅ u₁ u₂` $\subseteq$ `Grid X` (per its docstring, the set $\mathcal{J}'$ defined at the start of Section 7.5.1). Let $C_{7.5.11}(a,n)\in\mathbb{R}_{\ge0}$ be the constant `Forest.C7_5_11 a n` (per its docstring, the constant used in `lower_oscillation_bound`, with value $2^{Zn/2-201a^3}$ in the blueprint, where $Z=$ `defaultZ a`). Then, as real numbers, $$C_{7.5.11}(a,n)\le d_{c(J),\,8D^{s(J)}}\big(\mathcal{Q}(u_1),\mathcal{Q}(u_2)\big).$$
